# Graph Report - batch-clone  (2026-11-14)

## Corpus Check
- 4701 files · ~10,818,390 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 107 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 33, .css 16, .entitlements 10)

## Summary
- 65342 nodes · 188592 edges · 1354 communities (1237 shown, 117 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7763 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e25515ba`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
